{"forms":[{"form":"plays along","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"playing along","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"played along","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"played along","tags":["past"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"James thought it was cruel for the fraternity members to make the pledge think his father had died suddenly and refused to play along.","type":"example"},{"text":"James played along with their cruel trick.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To take part in a charade, deception, or practical joke."],"tags":["idiomatic"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I knew that my blind date was an April Fools trick, but I played along anyway.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To participate in a joke or trick while hiding one's knowledge of its true nature."],"tags":["idiomatic"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I like to practise guitar by playing along with records.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To play a musical instrument in synchrony with a playback of a recording."],"tags":["idiomatic"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I have doubts about your plan, but I'll play along for now.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To cooperate or go along with a plan."],"tags":["US","idiomatic"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I haven't told him yet, I'm still playing along with his scheme.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To pretend to cooperate or go along with a plan."],"tags":["US","idiomatic"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The con man plays his victims along until he senses the moment to ask for money.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To manipulate (someone)."],"tags":["UK","idiomatic"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The children spent the day playing along the river.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Used other than figuratively or idiomatically: see play, along."]}],"sounds":[],"upstream_line":1109092,"upstream_offset":3931052864,"upstream_record_sha256":"7deebb493dfad48183c00d6ff8438b9378cb8c72ed680966f70dde2ffe3ed3a9","word":"play along"}
